=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def numberOfArithmeticSlices(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        total, n = 0, len(nums)
        dp = [defaultdict(int) for _ in nums]
        for i in range(1, n):
            for j in range(i):
                diff = nums[j] - nums[i]
                dp[i+1][diff] += dp[j][diff] + 1
                total += dp[j][diff]
        return total

```python
class Solution:
    def numberOfArithmeticSlices(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        total, n = 0, len(nums)
        dp = [defaultdict(int) for _ in nums]
        for i in range(1, n):
            for j in range(i):
                diff = nums[j] - nums[i]
                dp[i+1][diff] += dp[j][diff] + 1
                total += dp[j][diff]
        return total
```

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

def numberOfArithmeticSlices(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
    n = len(nums)
    total = 0
    dp = [defaultdict(int) for _ in range(n)]
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i):
            diff = nums[i] - nums[j]
            cnt = dp[j][diff]
            dp[i][diff] += cnt + 1
            total += cnt
    return total

```
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def numberOfArithmeticSlices(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        total, n = 0, len(nums)
        dp = [defaultdict(int) for _ in range(n)]
        for i in range(1, n):
            for j in range(i):
                diff = nums[j] - nums[i]
                dp[i][diff] += dp[j][diff] + 1
                total += dp[j][diff]
        return total
```

```
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def numberOfArithmeticSlices(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        total, n = 0, len(nums)
        dp = [defaultdict(int) for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(1, n):
            for j in range(i):
                diff = nums[j] - nums[i]
                dp[i][diff] += dp[j][diff] + 1

        return total
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def numberOfArithmeticSlices(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        total, n = 0, len(nums)
        dp = [defaultdict(int) for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(1, n):
            for j in range(i):
                diff = nums[j] - nums[i]
                dp[i][diff] += dp[j][diff] + 1
                total += dp[j][diff]

        return total
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from collections import defaultdict

class Solution:
    def numberOfArithmeticSlices(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        total, n = 0, len(nums)
        dp = [defaultdict(int) for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(1, n):
            for j in range(i):
                diff = nums[j] - nums[i]
                dp[i][diff] += dp[j][diff] + 1
                total += dp[j][diff]

        return total
